## `ALB-2310-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-02`

- **Country:** `ALB` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** Explosion Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** 4M1E Engineering Change (ECN) Containment Approval & 8D/CAPA Root Cause Rectification SOP for Float Glass Tin Bath Hydrogen Atmosphere & Molten Tin Explosion Safety Incidents

Initial gas readings must be recorded with accuracy ±0.1% for H2 and ±0.1% for O2.
- **Key tolerance:** Response time to log incident: ≤ 10 minutes from detection. Initial gas readings must be recorded with accuracy ±0.1% for H2 and ±0.1% for O2.
